  GET http://127.0.0.1:56103/cdn-cgi/local/explorer/api/local/workers - local Workers and bindings
  POST http://127.0.0.1:56103/cdn-cgi/local/explorer/api/local/scheduled?worker=<name> - invoke a Worker's scheduled handler; cron is required (see the OpenAPI schema for the full request)
  GET http://127.0.0.1:56103/cdn-cgi/local/explorer/api/storage/kv/namespaces - KV namespaces
  GET http://127.0.0.1:56103/cdn-cgi/local/explorer/api/d1/database - D1 databases
  GET http://127.0.0.1:56103/cdn-cgi/local/explorer/api/r2/buckets - R2 buckets
  GET http://127.0.0.1:56103/cdn-cgi/local/explorer/api/workers/durable_objects/namespaces - Durable Object namespaces
  GET http://127.0.0.1:56103/cdn-cgi/local/explorer/api/workflows - Workflows
  POST http://127.0.0.1:56103/cdn-cgi/local/explorer/api/local/observability/query - run a read-only SQL query (SELECT/WITH only) over captured request traces and console logs. Tables: spans, logs (read attributes via json(attributes)). Example:
    curl -X POST http://127.0.0.1:56103/cdn-cgi/local/explorer/api/local/observability/query -H 'Content-Type: application/json' -d '{"sql":"SELECT service, name, outcome, duration_ms FROM spans WHERE parent_id IS NULL LIMIT 20"}'
  POST http://127.0.0.1:56103/cdn-cgi/local/explorer/api/local/observability/clear - clear all captured traces and logs
If the routes above don't cover what you need, fetch the full OpenAPI schema (large - use only as a last resort):
  GET http://127.0.0.1:56103/cdn-cgi/local/explorer/api - OpenAPI schema
